Let's analyze previous SOTA LP approach more. It may be more globally effective than gradient. Could implement a simplified primal-dual multiplicative weights without full LP:
Given a, M. We want b maximizing sum s.t. T b ≤ M. This is a linear program. Because T is nonnegative and b≥0. We can solve via "Dantzig-Wolfe" or use scipy linprog for moderate n with warm start? Maybe not closed-form. But we can approximate with iterative fitting:
Initialize b = a. Repeat:
- Find violations v_k = (T b)_k / M. Increase b_j more when? Actually to maximize sum under constraints, at optimum for positive b_j, dual T^T y =1. We can keep y. Update b_j *= b_j / (T^T y)_j? Update y_k *= y_k / (T b)_k? This is multiplicative weights for zero-sum game, converges to approximate optimal. Then scale b to sum n. If new a gives lower M, keep. This is similar to "Gutknecht" / "column generation". Could be more robust.

Let's formalize. Primal max 1^T b s.t. T b ≤ M 1, b≥0. Dual min M 1^T y s.t. T^T y ≥ 1, y≥0. If we run multiplicative:
b_j ← b_j / (T^T y)_j (or exp) until T^T y ≈1; y_k ← y_k / (T b)_k / M until T b≈M. At equilibrium, b feasible, y feasible, and complementary b_j (T^T y_j -1)=0. Not exact but good. Let's derive multiplicative primal-dual for this LP. At current f, M. We want max 1^Tb. Let constraints indexed k. We can use "Level method": maintain y_k ≥0. Update b_j = max(0, b_j / max(1, T^T y_j/M?)). Let's use a known algorithm for dense nonnegative LP: multiplicative weights:
Initialize b = f (sum S), y = uniform/(?) maybe.
For t steps:
- Compute u = T b / M (violations, ≤1 if feasible)
- Compute v = T^T y (constraints)

Let's design functions:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    size=1
    while size<m: size*=2
    return size

def fft_convolution(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    return c[:2*n-1]

def correlate_shift_weights(w, a):
    # V[j]=sum_{m=0}^{n-1} w[j+m] a[m]
    n=len(a)
    size=next_pow2(len(w)+n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    # Need ifft of fw * conj(fa)
    # rfft of real; conj fine. Use ifft full? np.fft.irfft? Multiplication not conjugate symmetric; result complex? For real w,a, correlation result real. If use rfft, product may not be Hermitian? Use np.fft.fft for safety (slower). Could use rfft and set? Simpler use np.fft.fft. For size up to 131k, complex FFT okay. Use np.fft.fft(fw)*np.conj(fa) then ifft.
    return np.fft.ifft(fw * np.conj(fa)).real[:n]
```

Can use rfft for both and irfft? The product of two rfft spectra not Hermitian generally, but ifft of full product via irfft requires conjugate symmetry; not guaranteed. Use full fft.

```
def fast_score_n(c):
```

```
def soft_weights(c, n, beta):
    y = c * n
    m = y.max()
    shifted = beta*(y-m)
    # underflow: if all shifted < -700, set max
    exp=np.exp(shifted - max shifted? shifted<=0; max 0)
    s=exp.sum()
    if s<=0: exp=zeros; exp[argmax(y)]=1
    else exp/=s
    return exp
```

```
def logit_grad(p, weights):
   V=correlate(weights,p)
   dot=np.dot(p,V)
   g=V - p*dot
   return g*len(p) # scale
```

Adam:
```
def adam_update(u, grad, state, lr):
   m = state['m']; v=state['v']; t=state['t']+1
   m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad
   v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
   u_new = u - lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
   u_new -= u_new.mean()
   state['u']=u_new; state['m']=m; state['v']=v; state['t']=t
   return u_new
```
No closures; state dict.

But helper top-level no closures; can pass.

Upsampling parent to n:
```
def resample_array(arr, n):
    m=len(arr)
    if m==n: return arr.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr)
    y=np.maximum(y,0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s<1e-12: y=np.ones(n)
    else y/=y.sum()
    return y
```
If arr has zeros, interpolation fine. But maybe linear interpolation introduces small negative? clip.

Correlation V[j]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1} w[j+m] a[m]. This is linear convolution of w_rev? Let define W_rev[t]=w[2n-2 - t]. Linear convolution r = W_rev * a. Then r[?]. Compute r[s]=∑_u W_rev[u] a[s-u]=∑_u w[2n-2-u] a[s-u]. Let m=s-u -> u=s-m. r[s]=∑_m w[2n-2-s+m] a[m]. We want V[j]=∑_m w[j+m] a[m]. Set 2n-2-s = j => s=2n-2-j. So V[j] = r[2n-2-j]. Convolution length ≤3n-2. We can compute with rfft: conv = irfft(rfft(W_rev)*rfft(a), size). This uses rfft and real output. This may be faster (half size and real). Let's use this to optimize.

```
def convolution_two(w, a):  # full linear convolution
   size=next_pow2(len(w)+len(a)-1)
   return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w,size)*np.fft.rfft(a,size), size)
```

j=1 => s=1. r[1]=rev_w[1]a0 + rev_w[0]a1 = w1 a0 + w2 a1 = V1 good.

Implementation:

```
def real_conv2(x,y):
    n = len(x)+len(y)-1
    size=1
    while size<n: size <<=1
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,size)*np.fft.rfft(y,size), size)[:n]

def self_conv_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=1
    needed=2*n-1
    while size<needed: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:needed]

def peak_contribution(weights, p):
    n=len(p)
    needed=len(weights)+n-1
    W=next_pow2(needed)

    conv=np.fft.irfft(fw_rev*fp, W)
```

Softmax with u:
```
def softmax_from_u(u):
    u = u - u.max()
    e=np.exp(u)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```

Detailed optimize:
```
def optimize_logits(p_init, budget, start_offset=0, seed=0, description="", best_score=np.inf, best_p=None):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget
    n=len(p_init)
    p=normalize_prob(p_init)
    if not finite: p=ones
    u=np.log(p+1e-300); u-=u.mean(); u=np.clip(u,-80,80); u-=u.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
    lr_start=0.06; lr_end=0.004
    beta_start=2.0; beta_end=120.0
    # current
    c=self_convolution_fft(p)
    fs=2*n*c.max()
    u_good=u.copy(); p_good=p.copy(); fs_good=fs
    last_good = start
    last_exact = start
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    restart_scale=0.25
    stall_limit = max(45.0, budget/15.0)
    no_fast_improve=0
    # if initial fs better than best? maybe exact if small
    if fs < best_score - 1e-12:
        # if n<=6000 or time, exact else set best fast
        if n <= 8000:
           sc=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
           if finite: best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy()
        else:
           # trust fast for large? but global best maybe exact? we can set best_score=fs,best_p=p only if n? We need consistent scores. For comparing different n, fast is same metric. Use fast best separately? We can maintain global by fast to avoid exact. Simpler: use fast_score as objective globally. Then no need exact. But maybe print exact for final. Let's do global best by fast, and final exact optional.
```

Now, restarts and random: Need ensure `make_smooth_random` uses `np.cos(2π * k * (t + phase)/n)`. If phases large, okay.

```
def make_smooth_random(n, rng, n_modes=24, smoothness=1.2):
    t = np.arange(n)
    z = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1, n_modes+1):
        amp = 1.0 / (k ** smoothness)
        a = rng.normal(0.0, amp)
        b = rng.normal(0.0, amp)
        ph = 2*np.pi*k*(t/n)
        z += a*np.cos(ph) + b*np.sin(ph)
    # remove mean, normalize
    z -= z.mean()
    std = z.std()
    if std < 1e-12: return np.zeros(n)
    return z/std
```
Scale later. This gives unit std.

`lowpass_keep` directly:
```
def lowpass_filter(a, n_modes):
    n=len(a)
    size=next_pow2(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    if fa.size > n_modes+1: fa[n_modes+1:] = 0
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa, size)[:n]
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<1e-12: out=np.ones(n)
    else out/=s
    return out
```
Use before large init? A profile from previous large already smooth. Applying lowpass may lose small beneficial features; but can help. Maybe do very gentle after upscaling: keep first 1% of modes. For n=16384, n_modes=256. That retains modes <0.015 cycles/sample? Actually freq k/size; k=256 out of 8192 ~0.031 cycles/sample; okay. If profile has features at higher, may remove. Parent likely low freq. Use only if previous n < current n and parent from direct not smooth? I'd apply with n_modes=max(32, min(512, n//64)). For 16k n//64=256. Good.

But if the best sequence has high-frequency components that help reduce max, lowpass could hurt. Is finite-n optimum smooth? As n grows, if bonuses from high freq vanish/oscillate, smooth likely. For 16k, high freq may be finite artifacts. Should be okay. But to be safe, maybe blend original and low-pass: `p = 0.7*low +0.3*orig`. Let's do that.

```
def optimize_logits(p_init, budget, seed, description=""):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget
    n = len(p_init)
    p = p_init.copy()
    p = np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: p=np.ones(n); s=n
    else: p = p/s
    u = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n); tstep=0
    ln=np.log(16384?) no.
    c = self_convolution_fft(p)
    fs = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    best_local_score = fs; best_local_p = p.copy()
    u_good = u.copy(); p_good = p.copy(); fs_good = fs
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    last_good = start
    restart_scale = 0.2
    stall_limit = max(40.0, budget/12.0)
    it=0
    eval_best_local? no exact.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic()-start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed/budget)
        beta = 2.0 + (120.0-2.0)*(min(1.0, elapsed/(0.75*budget)))**0.7
        lr = 0.06 * (0.004/0.06)**frac
        # weights
        w = soft_weights(c,n,beta)
        V = shifted_correlation(w,p)
        dot = float(np.dot(p,V))
        grad = n * (V - p*dot)
        # NaN guard
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
             # revert
        # Adam
        tstep +=1
        m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
        v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad*grad
        step = lr * m / (np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
        # optionally clip step magnitude to 0.5?
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
        u = u - step
        u = u - np.mean(u)
        u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
        u = u - np.mean(u)
        p = softmax_from_u(u)
        c = self_convolution_fft(p)
        fs = 2.0*n*c.max()
        it +=1
        if fs < fs_good - 1e-12:
             fs_good=fs; u_good=u.copy(); p_good=p.copy(); last_good=time.monotonic(); restart_scale=min(0.6, restart_scale*0.95?)
        if fs < best_local_score - 1e-10:
             best_local_score=fs; best_local_p=p.copy()
        # Print every ? maybe if it % 2000 ==0 or significant
        if it % 1000 ==0:
             print(f"{description} it={it} fs={fs:.8f} beta={beta:.1f} lr={lr:.5f}")
        # Restart
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now - last_good > stall_limit:
             noise = make_smooth_random(n, rng, n_modes=max(8, min(64, n//256)))
             noise *= (np.std(u)+0.2) * restart_scale
             u = u_good + noise
             u = np.clip(u - np.mean(u), -80, 80)
             p = softmax_from_u(u)
             c = self_convolution_fft(p)
             fs = 2*n*c.max()
             # if too bad, revert
             if fs > fs_good + 0.05:
                 u = u_good.copy(); p=p_good.copy(); c=self_convolution_fft(p); fs=fs_good
             else:
                 m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); tstep=0
                 last_good=now
                 restart_scale = min(0.9, restart_scale*1.2)
                 print restart
        # safety if very close to deadline, break and final evaluate?
    # maybe final: if best_local_p is p_good? ensure
    return best_local_p, best_local_score
```

```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(512)
    arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): arr=np.ones(512)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<0.01: arr=np.ones_like(arr)
    else: arr=arr/s
    return arr
```
If parent length maybe 2535. Good.

Resampling:
```
def resample_prob(arr, n):
    m=len(arr)
    if m==n: out=arr.copy()
    elif m==1: out=np.full(n, arr[0])
    else:
       old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       out=np.interp(new, old, arr)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<1e-12: out=np.ones(n)
    else: out=out/s
    return out
```

`lowpass_mix`:
```
def lowpass_mix(p, keep_modes):
   n=len(p)
   lp=lowpass_filter(p, keep_modes)
   blended=0.75*lp+0.25*p
   return normalize_prob(blended)
```

For random-like p_init:
```
def random_mixed_profile(base, n, rng, mix=0.5, sigma=1.0, n_modes=48):
    base_n=resample_prob(base,n)
    base_n = lowpass_mix(base_n, max(32,min(512,n//64)))
    noise=make_smooth_random(n, rng, n_modes)
    random_part = np.exp(np.clip(noise*sigma, -6,6))
    random_part /= random_part.sum()
    p = mix*random_part + (1-mix)*base_n
    return normalize_prob(p)
```

Detailed `make_smooth_random`: `n_modes` maybe bigger than n/2? cap.
```
def make_smooth_random(n, rng, n_modes=32):
    if n_modes > n//2: n_modes=max(1,n//2)
    t=np.arange(n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,n_modes+1):
       amp = 1.0 / (k**0.8)
       ph=2*np.pi*k*(t/n)
       z += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(ph)+rng.normal()*np.sin(ph))
    z -= z.mean()
    std = z.std()
    if std ==0: return zeros
    return z/std
```
Using `t/n` continuous [0,1). Modes integer cycles over n. Good.

`random_mixed_profile(base, n, rng, mix, sigma, n_modes)`:
```
base_n = resample_prob(base,n)
keep=max(32,min(256,n//64))
base_n = lowpass_mix(base_n, keep)
noise=make_smooth_random(n,rng,n_modes)
rand = np.exp(np.clip(noise*sigma, -6.0, 6.0))
s=rand.sum(); rand/=s
p = mix*rand + (1-mix)*base_n
return normalize_prob(p)
```
Mix 0.4 maybe.

Let's design `project_logit_modes(u, n, K)`:
```
def project_logit_to_modes(u, n, K):
    if K <=0: return u - u.mean()
    size = next_pow2(n)
    F = np.fft.fft(u, size)
    # keep center and first K positive/negative
    # frequencies indices 0..size-1; positive:1..K, negative:size-K..size-1
    if K < size//2:
        mask_keep = np.ones(size, dtype=bool)? setting zero each time alloc. Better:
        F[1:K+1]?? can't assign with K maybe; use slices.
        F[K+1:size-K] = 0.0  # if K+1 <= size-K
    u_out = np.fft.ifft(F).real[:n]
    return u_out
```

```
def prepare_profile(base, n, rng, mix=0.0, sigma=1.0, mode_limit=0):
    p = resample_prob(base, n)
    keep = max(32, min(256, n//64))
    p = lowpass_mix(p, keep)
    if mix > 0.0:
        noise = make_smooth_random(n, rng, max(16, min(128, n//64)))
        rnd = np.exp(np.clip(noise*sigma, -5,5)); rnd/=rnd.sum()
        p = mix*rnd + (1-mix)*p
        p = normalize_prob(p)
    return p
```

```
def random_smooth_profile(base,n,rng,mix,sigma):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   # random polynomial degree up to 6
   for d in range(0,7): amp=rng.normal(0, sigma/(d+1)); z += amp*(x-0.5)**d
   # add random smooth cosine/sin with integer half-wave? cos(pi*k*x), sin(pi*k*x) k=1..8
   for k in range(1,9): amp=rng.normal(0,sigma/(1+k)); ph=...; z += amp*(np.cos(np.pi*k*x)+np.sin(...))
   # add endpoint ramp? already polynomial odd.
   rand=np.exp(np.clip(z, -6,6)); normalize; mix with base
```

```
for d in range(poly_deg+1):

```

```
def make_random_smooth_vector(n, rng, sigma=1.0):
   x=np.linspace(0.1,1.0,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for d,u in enumerate((x-0.5)**d?) to avoid recompute? Could compute iteratively.
   # polynomial powers:
   u=x-0.5
   pow_u=np.ones(n)
   for d in range(0,7):
       z += rng.normal(0.0, sigma/(1.0+d)) * pow_u
       if d<6: pow_u *= u
   for k in range(1,9):
       a=rng.normal(0.0, sigma/(1.0+k)); b=...
       z += a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x)+b*np.sin(np.pi*k*x)
   z -= z.mean(); std=...; return z/std
```
This is nonperiodic and allows asymmetry. Use for restarts and random profiles. It returns unit std.

```
def add_laplacian_to_grad(grad, u, weight):
    n = len(u)
    if weight == 0.0 or n <= 1: return grad
    pen = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 2:
       pen[0] = u[0] - u[1]  # gradient of (u0-u1)^2? derivative 2(u0-u1)? Hmm.
       pen[1] = u[1] - u[0]
    else:
       pen[0] = 2*u[0] - u[1]
       pen[-1] = 2*u[-1] - u[-2]
       pen[1:-1] = 2*u[1:-1] - u[:-2] - u[2:]
    grad += weight * pen
    return grad
```
Penalty R = u0^2? For first difference penalty with endpoints? Our pen approx 2*Laplace. It's fine.

Now, random profile generator. Let's refine:
```
def make_random_smooth_vector(n, rng, sigma=1.0):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = x - 0.5
    z = np.zeros(n)
    pow_u = np.ones(n)
    for d in range(7):
        z += rng.normal(0.0, sigma/(1.0+d)) * pow_u
        if d != 6:
            pow_u = pow_u * u
    for k in range(1, 9):
        a = rng.normal(0.0, sigma/(1.0+k))
        b = rng.normal(0.0, sigma/(1.0+k))
        th = np.pi * k * x
        z += a*np.cos(th) + b*np.sin(th)
    z -= z.mean()
    sd = z.std()
    if sd < 1e-12: return z
    return z/sd
```
This vector may have large values due polynomial x^6; normalized std 1. Good.

For restart noise, use this. For random profile: `p_rand = exp(clip(noise*sigma, -5,5))`. Since noise std1, sigma=1 gives p range e^±5. Good.

`add_laplacian_to_grad`: Modifies grad in place? To avoid allocate pen? We'll allocate. Use if weight <=1e-12 return.
```
def add_laplacian_to_grad(grad, u, weight):
   n=len(u)
   if weight ==0.0 or n<=2? handle
   if n==2:
      d=u[0]-u[1]
      grad[0]+=weight*d
      grad[1]-=weight*d
   else:
      pen0 = 2*u[0]-u[1]
      grad[0]+=weight*pen0
      grad[-1]+=weight*(2*u[-1]-u[-2])
      grad[1:-1] += weight*(2*u[1:-1] - u[:-2] - u[2:])
```
No allocate full. Good. But `grad[1:-1] += ...` creates temp. Fine.

`optimize_logits` details:
Parameters: `p_init, budget, seed=0, description="", mode_random? no, smooth_start=0.0, smooth_end=0.0, beta_start=3.0, beta_end=200.0, lr_start=0.08, lr_end=0.005`.
Inside if `budget <= 30` return normalized p, fast.

Normalize p.
Initial u: `u = np.log(p + 1e-300)`, `u -= u.mean()`, `u = np.clip(u, -80,80)`, `u -= u.mean()`.
If `smooth_start>0` and want smooth initial, maybe not.
Compute c, fs.
Initialize `best_local_score=fs`, `best_local_p=p.copy()`, `u_good=u.copy()`, etc.

`restart_scale=0.25`; `max_restart=0.9`.
`stall_limit = max(40.0, budget/10.0)`; maybe for larger budget 200s, okay.

`m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)`.
Loop:
```
while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
    now=time.monotonic(); elapsed=now-start; frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(budget,1e-6))
    beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*min(1.0, elapsed/(0.75*budget))**0.7
    lr = lr_start * (lr_end/lr_start) ** frac
    sw = smooth_start + (smooth_end - smooth_start) * frac
    w = soft_weights(c,n,beta)
    V = shifted_correlation(w,p)
    dot = float(np.dot(p,V))
    grad = n * (V - p*dot)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        # revert
        u = u_good.copy(); p=p_good.copy(); c=self_convolution_fft(p); fs=fs_good; m=zeros; continue
    if sw > 1e-8:
        add_laplacian_to_grad(grad,u,sw)
    t_adam +=1
    m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad
    v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad*grad
    step = lr * m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
    # clip step to [-1,1] to avoid huge
    step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
    u = u - step
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    p = softmax_from_u(u)
    c = self_convolution_fft(p)
    fs = 2.0 * float(n * np.max(c))
    it +=1
    if fs < fs_good - 1e-11:
        fs_good=fs; u_good=u.copy(); p_good=p.copy(); last_good=now; restart_scale=max(0.1, restart_scale*0.98)
    if fs < best_local_score - 1e-9:
        best_local_score=fs; best_local_p=p.copy()
    if now - last_print > 20.0 or (it % 5000 ==0 and now-last_print>5.0):
        print(...); last_print=now
    if now - last_good > stall_limit:
        noise = make_random_smooth_vector(n, rng, 1.0)
        scale = (float(np.std(u)) + 0.1) * restart_scale
        u_new = u_good + noise * scale
        u_new -= np.mean(u_new); u_new=np.clip(u_new,-80,80); u_new-=np.mean(u_new)
        p_new=softmax_from_u(u_new)
        c_new=self_convolution_fft(p_new)
        fs_new = 2*n*max(c_new)
        if fs_new < fs_good + 0.05:
            u=u_new; p=p_new; c=c_new; fs=fs_new
            m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
            last_good=now
            restart_scale=min(0.9, restart_scale*1.15)
            print restart
        else:
            # maybe reduce scale? revert
            u=u_good.copy(); p=p_good.copy(); c=self_convolution_fft(p); fs=fs_good
            m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
            last_good=now
            restart_scale=max(0.05, restart_scale*0.7)
```

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step functions.

Strategy:
- The objective is scale-invariant, so all search arrays are kept as probability
  vectors (sum = 1).
- Use a mirror-descent / Adam optimizer in logit space (softmax parameterization),
  which automatically keeps values nonnegative and sum-normalized.
- The nonsmooth max-convolution is smoothed by a log-sum-exp over scaled
  convolution coefficients.  A temperature is annealed from broad to sharp.
- Several restarts/multi-start phases are used, including random smooth
  non-periodic profiles and a short smoothed phase to find robust basins.
- Large lengths are used at the end because the asymptotic score is better,
  but exact external convolution of very large arrays is avoided during search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    size = 1
    while size < m:
        size <<= 1
    return size


def self_convolution_fft(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 1:
        out = a * a
        return out.reshape(-1)
    needed = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(needed)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return c[:needed].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def real_convolve_fft(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full linear convolution of real arrays using FFT."""
    needed = int(len(x) + len(y) - 1)
    size = next_pow2(needed)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fx * fy, size)[:needed].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def shifted_correlation(weights: np.ndarray, p: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Given weights w of length 2n-1 and p of length n, return V of length n:
        V[j] = sum_{m=0}^{n-1} w[j+m] * p[m]
    This is used for gradient computation.
    """
    n = int(len(p))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(weights[0] * p[0])])
    rev_w = weights[::-1]
    conv = real_convolve_fft(rev_w, p)
    # For n >= 2, this slice returns indices 2n-2, 2n-3, ..., n-1
    return conv[2 * n - 2 : n - 2 : -1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_prob(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return (a / s).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def fast_score(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Fast version of evaluate_sequence, using FFT convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = max(0.0, m)
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def softmax_from_u(u: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    u = np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
    u = u - np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(u.shape, 1.0 / max(1, u.size), dtype=np.float64)
    p = e / s
    # Remove tiny roundoff error.
    s2 = float(p.sum())
    if s2 > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(s2):
        p /= s2
    return p.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def soft_weights(c: np.ndarray, n: int, beta: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Compute softmax weights over the scaled convolution coefficients.
    y = n * c, so max(y) is about half the objective score.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    beta = float(beta)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c.copy()

    y = c * float(n)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-1e300, posinf=-1e300, neginf=-1e300)
    max_y = float(np.max(y))
    if not np.isfinite(max_y):
        w = np.zeros_like(y)
        w[int(np.argmax(y))] = 1.0
        return w

    shifted = beta * (y - max_y)
    # Exp of a very negative number underflows safely.
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(y)
        w[int(np.argmax(y))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        w = np.zeros_like(y)
        w[int(np.argmax(y))] = 1.0
    return w.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def add_laplacian_to_grad(grad: np.ndarray, u: np.ndarray, weight: float) -> None:
    """
    Add gradient of a curvature regularizer approximating sum(second difference)^2.
    It discourages high-frequency wiggle but does not force perfect periodicity.
    """
    w = float(weight)
    if w <= 1e-12:
        return
    n = int(len(u))
    if n <= 1:
        return
    if n == 2:
        d = float(u[0] - u[1])
        grad[0] += w * d
        grad[1] -= w * d
        return

    grad[0] += w * (2.0 * u[0] - u[1])
    grad[-1] += w * (2.0 * u[-1] - u[-2])
    if n > 2:
        grad[1:-1] += w * (2.0 * u[1:-1] - u[:-2] - u[2:])


def make_random_smooth_vector(n: int, rng: np.random.Generator, sigma: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Generate a smooth non-periodic random vector with mean zero and std one.
    Uses low-degree polynomials and low-frequency trigonometric terms.
    """
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = x - 0.5

    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Polynomial part: good for endpoint asymmetry and ramps.
    pow_u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for d in range(7):
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, sigma / (1.0 + d)))
        z += amp * pow_u
        if d < 6:
            pow_u = pow_u * u

    # Smooth trigonometric part (not forced periodic over [0,1] with integer
    # full cycles; uses cos(pi*k*x), sin(pi*k*x)).
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = sigma / (1.0 + k)
        a = float(rng.normal(0.0, amp))
        b = float(rng.normal(0.0, amp))
        th = np.pi * float(k) * x
        z += a * np.cos(th) + b * np.sin(th)

    z -= float(z.mean())
    sd = float(z.std())
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (z / sd).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def lowpass_filter(a: np.ndarray, keep_modes: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Gentle FFT low-pass filter, then normalize as probability."""
    a = normalize_prob(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    size = next_pow2(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    k = int(keep_modes)
    if k > 0 and k + 1 < fa.size:
        fa[k + 1:] = 0.0
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa, size)[:n].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    else:
        out /= s
    return out


def lowpass_mix(a: np.ndarray, keep_modes: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Blend original profile with a lightly low-passed version.
    Removes numerical / finite-n artifacts while retaining the original shape.
    """
    p = normalize_prob(a)
    lp = lowpass_filter(p, keep_modes)
    out = 0.75 * lp + 0.25 * p
    return normalize_prob(out)


def resample_prob(arr: np.ndarray, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Resample a probability vector to length n on the uniform [0,1] grid."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(arr.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == 1:
        v = float(np.nan_to_num(arr[0], nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0))
        v = max(0.0, v)
        if v <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return np.full(n, v / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        out = arr.copy()
    else:
        old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
        new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        clean = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        out = np.interp(new, old, clean)

    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out /= s
    return out.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def prepare_profile(
    base: np.ndarray,
    n: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator = None,
    mix: float = 0.0,
    sigma: float = 1.0,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Prepare an initial probability vector of length n from base profile.
    If mix > 0 and rng is not None, mix in a smooth random profile.
    """
    p = resample_prob(base, n)
    keep = max(16, min(256, int(n) // 64))
    p = lowpass_mix(p, keep)

    mix = float(mix)
    if mix > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        noise = make_random_smooth_vector(n, rng, sigma=1.0)
        # exp of clipped noise gives a positive smooth profile.
        rnd = np.exp(np.clip(noise * float(sigma), -6.0, 6.0))
        s = float(rnd.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-12:
            rnd /= s
        else:
            rnd = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        mix = min(1.0, max(0.0, mix))
        p = (1.0 - mix) * p + mix * rnd
        p = normalize_prob(p)
    else:
        p = normalize_prob(p)

    return p


def get_parent_sequence() -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Try to recover the previously found sequence from the global environment.
    Fallback to a harmless uniform profile.
    """
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        raw = None

    if raw is None:
        return np.ones(2535, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(2535, dtype=np.float64)

    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(2535, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-9:
        return np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return (arr / s).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def optimize_logits(
    p_init: np.ndarray,
    budget: float,
    seed: int = 0,
    description: str = "",
    smooth_start: float = 0.0,
    smooth_end: float = 0.0,
    beta_start: float = 3.0,
    beta_end: float = 220.0,
    lr_start: float = 0.07,
    lr_end: float = 0.004,
) -> tuple:
    """
    Optimize a probability vector in logit/softmax space.

    Returns (best_p, best_fast_score).
    """
    p = normalize_prob(p_init)
    n = int(p.size)
    budget = float(budget)
    if n == 0 or budget <= 20.0:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget

    u = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    u -= float(u.mean())
    u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
    u -= float(u.mean())

    c = self_convolution_fft(p)
    fs = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(fs):
        fs = 2.0

    best_local_score = fs
    best_local_p = p.copy()

    u_good = u.copy()
    p_good = p.copy()
    fs_good = fs

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    m_mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_adam = 0

    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999

    last_improve = start
    last_restart = start - 1000.0
    last_print = start
    best_print_score = fs

    restart_scale = 0.25
    stall_limit = max(45.0, budget / 18.0)
    restart_cooldown = 20.0

    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-6, budget))

        # Anneal temperature.  Use a slightly delayed ramp so the final part
        # is sharp.
        beta_frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-6, 0.75 * budget))
        beta = float(beta_start) + float(beta_end - beta_start) * (beta_frac ** 0.7)
        lr = float(lr_start) * (float(lr_end) / float(lr_start)) ** frac
        sw = float(smooth_start) + float(smooth_end - smooth_start) * frac

        w = soft_weights(c, n, beta)
        V = shifted_correlation(w, p)

        dot = float(np.dot(p, V))
        grad = float(n) * (V - p * dot)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            # Revert to the best known local state.
            u = u_good.copy()
            p = p_good.copy()
            c = self_convolution_fft(p)
            fs = fs_good
            m_mom.fill(0.0)
            v_mom.fill(0.0)
            t_adam = 0
            continue

        if sw > 1e-10:
            add_laplacian_to_grad(grad, u, sw)

        t_adam += 1
        m_mom = b1 * m_mom + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v_mom = b2 * v_mom + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)

        denom1 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - (b1 ** t_adam))
        denom2 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - (b2 ** t_adam))
        m_hat = m_mom / denom1
        v_hat = v_mom / denom2

        step = lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)

        u = u - step
        u -= float(u.mean())
        u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
        u -= float(u.mean())

        p = softmax_from_u(u)
        c = self_convolution_fft(p)
        fs_new = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(fs_new):
            u = u_good.copy()
            p = p_good.copy()
            c = self_convolution_fft(p)
            fs_new = fs_good

        p = normalize_prob(p)
        fs = fs_new
        it += 1

        if fs < fs_good - 1e-11:
            fs_good = fs
            u_good = u.copy()
            p_good = p.copy()
            last_improve = now

        if fs < best_local_score - 1e-9:
            best_local_score = fs
            best_local_p = p.copy()

        if now - last_print > 25.0 or (
            it % 5000 == 0 and fs < best_print_score - 1e-7
        ):
            print(
                f"{description} n={n} it={it} fast={fs:.9f} "
                f"beta={beta:.1f} lr={lr:.5f} sw={sw:.3f}",
                flush=True
            )
            last_print = now
            best_print_score = fs

        if now - last_improve > stall_limit and now - last_restart > restart_cooldown:
            noise = make_random_smooth_vector(n, rng, sigma=1.0)
            u_std = float(np.std(u))
            if not np.isfinite(u_std) or u_std < 1e-9:
                u_std = 1.0
            scale = u_std * restart_scale
            u_try = u_good + noise * scale
            u_try -= float(u_try.mean())
            u_try = np.clip(u_try, -80.0, 80.0)
            u_try -= float(u_try.mean())

            p_try = softmax_from_u(u_try)
            p_try = normalize_prob(p_try)
            c_try = self_convolution_fft(p_try)
            fs_try = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c_try))

            last_restart = now
            accept_tol = 0.02 + 0.02 * (1.0 - frac)
            if np.isfinite(fs_try) and fs_try < fs_good + accept_tol:
                u = u_try
                p = p_try
                c = c_try
                fs = fs_try
                m_mom.fill(0.0)
                v_mom.fill(0.0)
                t_adam = 0
                restart_scale = min(0.85, restart_scale * 1.12)
                if fs < fs_good - 1e-11:
                    fs_good = fs
                    u_good = u.copy()
                    p_good = p.copy()
                    last_improve = now
                if fs < best_local_score - 1e-9:
                    best_local_score = fs
                    best_local_p = p.copy()
                print(f"{description} restart accepted: fast={fs:.9f} scale={scale:.3f}", flush=True)
            else:
                # Revert to best local state, and slightly reduce perturbation.
                u = u_good.copy()
                p = p_good.copy()
                c = self_convolution_fft(p)
                fs = fs_good
                m_mom.fill(0.0)
                v_mom.fill(0.0)
                t_adam = 0
                restart_scale = max(0.08, restart_scale * 0.82)
                print(f"{description} restart rejected: fast={fs:.9f} tried={fs_try:.9f}", flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # A final harmless evaluation in case the loop stopped just before an update.
        if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            c = self_convolution_fft(p)
            fs = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
            if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_local_score:
                best_local_score = fs
                best_local_p = p.copy()

    best_local_p = normalize_prob(best_local_p)
    return best_local_p, best_local_score


def propose_candidate() -> list:
    """
    Entry point.  Runs multi-phase logit-space search with smooth restarts and
    gradually larger lengths, returning the best nonnegative coefficient list.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = 2330.0  # leave some margin inside the 2400s limit.

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    current_profile = parent.copy()
    current_score = fast_score(current_profile)

    best_p = parent.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    if np.isfinite(best_score):
        print(f"initial parent n={best_p.size} fast={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    else:
        best_p = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        best_score = 2.0
        current_profile = best_p.copy()
        current_score = 2.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: short smooth global search at moderate length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 1900.0:
        n = 4096
        budget = min(330.0, rem * 0.14)
        init = prepare_profile(best_p, n, rng=None, mix=0.0, sigma=1.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            init,
            budget,
            seed=101,
            description="smooth-4096",
            smooth_start=0.30,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=3.0,
            beta_end=180.0,
            lr_start=0.07,
            lr_end=0.004,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage):
            if s_stage < best_score:
                best_score = s_stage
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                print(f"new best from smooth-4096: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if s_stage < current_score + 0.004:
                current_profile = p_stage.copy()
                current_score = s_stage

    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: shorter random-smooth start at moderate length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 1600.0 and best_score > 1.512:
        n = 4096
        budget = min(130.0, rem * 0.06)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(202)
        init = prepare_profile(current_profile, n, rng=rng, mix=0.45, sigma=1.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            init,
            budget,
            seed=203,
            description="random-smooth-4096",
            smooth_start=0.35,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=4.0,
            beta_end=180.0,
            lr_start=0.07,
            lr_end=0.005,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage):
            if s_stage < best_score:
                best_score = s_stage
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                print(f"new best from random-smooth-4096: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if s_stage < current_score + 0.001:
                current_profile = p_stage.copy()
                current_score = s_stage

    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: main full search at larger length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 1100.0:
        n = 8192
        budget = min(860.0, rem * 0.43)
        init = prepare_profile(current_profile, n, rng=None, mix=0.0, sigma=1.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            init,
            budget,
            seed=304,
            description="full-8192",
            smooth_start=0.05,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=6.0,
            beta_end=240.0,
            lr_start=0.06,
            lr_end=0.003,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage):
            if s_stage < best_score:
                best_score = s_stage
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                print(f"new best from full-8192: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if s_stage < current_score + 0.003:
                current_profile = p_stage.copy()
                current_score = s_stage

    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: optional random多样化 full search at the same large length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 700.0 and best_score > 1.512:
        n = 8192
        budget = min(210.0, rem * 0.11)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(305)
        init = prepare_profile(current_profile, n, rng=rng, mix=0.35, sigma=1.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            init,
            budget,
            seed=306,
            description="random-full-8192",
            smooth_start=0.18,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=6.0,
            beta_end=220.0,
            lr_start=0.06,
            lr_end=0.004,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage):
            if s_stage < best_score:
                best_score = s_stage
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                print(f"new best from random-full-8192: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if s_stage < current_score + 0.001:
                current_profile = p_stage.copy()
                current_score = s_stage

    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: final large-length optimization.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 600.0:
        n = 16384
        budget = max(300.0, min(1000.0, rem - 120.0))
        init = prepare_profile(current_profile, n, rng=None, mix=0.0, sigma=1.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            init,
            budget,
            seed=407,
            description="full-16384",
            smooth_start=0.02,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=10.0,
            beta_end=260.0,
            lr_start=0.05,
            lr_end=0.003,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage):
            if s_stage < best_score:
                best_score = s_stage
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                print(f"new best from full-16384: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if s_stage < current_score + 0.001:
                current_profile = p_stage.copy()
                current_score = s_stage

    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6: short final polish on the current best profile.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 90.0:
        budget = min(250.0, rem - 40.0)
        p_stage, s_stage = optimize_logits(
            best_p,
            budget,
            seed=998,
            description="final-polish",
            smooth_start=0.0,
            smooth_end=0.0,
            beta_start=80.0,
            beta_end=280.0,
            lr_start=0.025,
            lr_end=0.001,
        )
        if np.isfinite(s_stage) and s_stage < best_score:
            best_score = s_stage
            best_p = p_stage.copy()
            print(f"new best from final-polish: {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Optional exact validation for modest sizes; skipped for very large n to
    # avoid spending the remaining budget on one O(n^2) direct convolution.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 12.0 and int(best_p.size) <= 9000:
        try:
            if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
                exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p])  # noqa: F821
                if np.isfinite(exact):
                    print(f"final exact validation score: {exact:.9f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Ensure the returned object is a plain list of finite nonnegative floats.
    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-9:
        out = np.ones(max(1, int(out.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.size)
    out = out / s

    # Keep sum = 1.  This avoids any evaluator clipping at 1000 and preserves
    # the scale-invariance of the objective.
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
